    return (i:gsub('.', function(j)
        if (j == '=') then
            return ''
        end

        local k, l = '', (('ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/'):find(j) - 1)

        for m = 6, 1, -1 do
            k = k .. (l % 2 ^ m - l % 2 ^ (m - 1) > 0 and '1' or '0')
        end

        return k
    end):gsub('%d%d%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?', function(j)
        if (#j ~= 8) then
            return ''
        end

        local k = 0

        for l = 1, 8 do
            k = k + (j:sub(l, l) == '1' and 2 ^ (8 - l) or 0)
        end

        return Oe(k)
    end))
end)
